 var APIURL = 'https://www.goeventz.com/api';
 var SITE_URL = "https:\/\/www.goeventz.com"+'/';
 function getlistingox(obj)
{
    $.post(SITE_URL+"getlistingbox",obj,function(data,status)
    {
      if(data!='')
      {
        if(obj['listingfor']=='recentviewcatpage')
        {
          $('.divforrecentview').html(data);

        }
        else if(obj['listingfor']=='likedevents')
        {
          $('.likedeventsview').html(data);
        }
      }
    }).fail(function(response) {
          //alert('Error: ' + response.responseText);
          
          });
  }
  function geteventsviewandlikes()
{
  var eventidlist = new Array();
  $('.event_listing_display_view').each(function()
  {
    eventidlist.push($(this).attr('id'));

  });

  if(eventidlist.length>0)
  {
     $.post(SITE_URL+'viewcounterall',{'eventidlist':eventidlist},function(data,status)
    {
      if(data.status=='success')
      {
        var eventdata = data.events;
        for(eventid in eventdata)
        {
          $('.evnetview'+eventid).html(eventdata[eventid].views);
          $('.evnetlikes'+eventid).html(eventdata[eventid].likes);
          if(eventdata[eventid].views > 0){
            $('.evnetview'+eventid).parent().removeClass('hidden');
          }
          if(eventdata[eventid].likes > 0){
            $('.evnetlikes'+eventid).parent().removeClass('hidden');
          }
        }

      }

    },'json').fail(function(response) {

         });

  }

}

<script type="text/javascript">
    
//mobile header
    $('.location_search').click(function(e){
      $('.searchlocationbox').show();
    });
    $('.mobileheader_location').click(function(e){
      e.stopPropagation();
    });
    $('.detailsearch_icon').click(function(e){
      $(this).toggleClass('active');
      $('#makespace').toggleClass('mh_space_search');
      $('.mobileheader_search').toggleClass('active');
    });
    var $window = $(window);
    $window.scroll(function(){
      if ($window.scrollTop() >= 102) {
        $('.mobileheader').addClass('mobileheader_fixed');
        $('.mobileheader_search').addClass('mobile_search_fixed');
        $('#makespace').addClass('makespace');
      }
      else {
        $('.mobileheader').removeClass('mobileheader_fixed');
        $('.mobileheader_search').removeClass('mobile_search_fixed');
        $('#makespace').removeClass('makespace');
      }
    });
    $(".mobilebar").click(function(e){
      e.stopPropagation();
      $(".nevigationparent").addClass('nevigationparentshow');
      $("body").addClass('bodylayers');
    });
    $('body').click(function() {
      $('.nevigationparent').removeClass('nevigationparentshow');
    });
    $(".nevigationparent").click(function(){
      $("body").removeClass('bodylayers');
      $(".nevigationparent").removeClass('nevigationparentshow');
    });
    //end mobile header
</script>

<script type="text/javascript">
  $(document).ready(function()
  {

    var stickyTop = $('#download_app_strip').offset().top;
      $(window).scroll(function() {
        var windowTop = $(window).scrollTop();
        if (stickyTop < windowTop) {
          $('.download_app_strip').addClass('fixed_download_app_strip')
        } else {
          $('.download_app_strip').removeClass('fixed_download_app_strip')
        }
      });
        $('.closestrip').click(function(){
            $('.download_app_strip').hide();
        });
    
  });
</script> <script src="https://www.goeventz.com/web/js/jquery.lazy.min.js" type="application/javascript"></script>
<script type="text/javascript">
lazyloadingimages();
function lazyloadingimages()
  {
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function(){
  var lazyloadImages;    
  if("IntersectionObserver" in window){
    lazyloadImages = document.querySelectorAll(".lazy");
    var imageObserver = new IntersectionObserver(function(entries, observer){
      entries.forEach(function(entry){
        if(entry.isIntersecting){
          var image = entry.target;
          image.src = image.dataset.src;
          image.classList.remove("lazy");
          image.parentNode.classList.remove("animated-background");
          imageObserver.unobserve(image);
        }
      });
    });
    lazyloadImages.forEach(function(image){
      imageObserver.observe(image);
    });
  } 
  else{  
    var lazyloadThrottleTimeout;
    lazyloadImages = document.querySelectorAll(".lazy");
    function lazyload(){
      if(lazyloadThrottleTimeout){
        clearTimeout(lazyloadThrottleTimeout);
      }    
      lazyloadThrottleTimeout = setTimeout(function(){
        var scrollTop = window.pageYOffset;
        lazyloadImages.forEach(function(img){
            if(img.offsetTop < (window.innerHeight + scrollTop)){
            alert('else');
              img.src = img.dataset.src;
              img.classList.remove('lazy');
              img.parentNode.classList.remove("animated-background");
            }
        });
        if(lazyloadImages.length == 0){ 
          document.removeEventListener("scroll", lazyload);
          window.removeEventListener("resize", lazyload);
          window.removeEventListener("orientationChange", lazyload);
        }
      }, 20);
    }
    document.addEventListener("scroll", lazyload);
    window.addEventListener("resize", lazyload);
    window.addEventListener("orientationChange", lazyload);
  }
})

  }

  
lazyloadingimagesajax();
function lazyloadingimagesajax()
{

  
   
    //end image lazy loding fade effect
    $('.lazy-image').lazy(
            {
            afterLoad: function(element) {
                var imageSrc = element.data('src');
                element.parent().removeClass('animated-background');
                element.removeClass('lazy-background');
            },
        });
}
 function isElementInViewport(el, $window) {
        var rect   = el.getBoundingClientRect();
        var offset = offset || 0;
        return ( rect.top <= $window.height() );
    }

</script>

<script type="text/javascript">
      $(document).ready(function()
      {
                var elemWidth, fitCount, varWidth = 0, ctr, 
            $menu = $("ul#menu"), $collectedSet;
        ctr = $menu.children().length;
        $menu.children().each(function() {
          varWidth += $(this).outerWidth();
        });
        collect();
        $(window).resize(collect);
        function collect() {
          elemWidth = $menu.width();
          fitCount = Math.floor((elemWidth / varWidth) * ctr) - 2;
          $menu.children().css({"display": "block", "width": "auto"});
          $collectedSet = $menu.children(":gt(" + fitCount + ")");
          $("#submenu").empty().append($collectedSet.clone());  
          $collectedSet.css({"display": "none", "width": "0"});
        }
        if($('#submenu').children().length > 0) {
          $('.collect').show();
        }
        // category ripple effect
$(".event_cat_mobile_list li").click(function (e) {
 $(".ripple").remove();
 $(this).prepend("<span class='ripple'></span>");
 $(".ripple").addClass("rippleEffect");
});
      });
    </script>

<script type="text/javascript">
         var owl = $('.image_carousel_bg .owl-carousel3');
      owl.owlCarousel({
        margin: 12,
        nav: true,
        loop:false,
        autoplay:true,
        slideTransition: 'linear',
            smartSpeed: 800,
        slideBy:1, 
        touchDrag:true,
        startPosition:0,
        responsiveClass:true,
        responsive: {
          0: {
            stagePadding:46,
            items: 1,
            margin:8,
            nav: false,
          },
          640: {
            stagePadding:35,
            items: 2,
            margin:8,
            nav: false,
          },
          768: {
            items: 3,
            nav: false,
            stagePadding:25,
          },
          1024: {
            items: 4,
          },
          1170: {
            items: 5,
          },
          1440: {
            items: 6,
          }
        }
      });
      var $window = $(window);
      function check_if_in_view() {
            var window_height = $window.height();
            var window_top_position = $window.scrollTop();
            var window_bottom_position = (window_top_position + window_height);
            var carousel_height = $('.image_carousel_bg').outerHeight();
            var carousel_top_position = $('.image_carousel_bg').offset().top;
            var carousel_bottom_position = (carousel_top_position + carousel_height);
        if((carousel_bottom_position >= window_top_position) && (carousel_top_position <= window_bottom_position)) {
          $('.image_carousel_bg').addClass('in-view');
            $('.owl-carousel3').trigger('play.owl.autoplay');
        } 
        else{
          $('.image_carousel_bg').removeClass('in-view');
          $('.owl-carousel3').trigger('stop.owl.autoplay');
        }
      }
      $window.on('scroll resize', check_if_in_view);
      $window.trigger('scroll');
    </script>

<script type="text/javascript">
$(document).ready(function()
  {

    makeeventlist();
    
  });
  function makeeventlist()
  {
    var cityname = 'India';
    var counter=0;
    var counternumber = $('input[name=countenumber]').val();
    $('.makecateventlist').each(function()
    {
      var catname = $(this).attr('id');
       $.post(SITE_URL+"makecateventlist",{'cityname':cityname,'catname':catname},function(data,status)
        {
           if(status=='success')
           {

            // console.log(catname);
             catname = catname.replace('&', 'and');
             catname = catname.replace(',', '_');
             catname = catname.split(' ').join('-');
             // alert(catname);
            $('.eventlist'+catname).html(data);
            lazyloadingimagesajax();

           }
        }).done(function() { //use this
            // alert("DONE!");
          
          }).fail(function(response) {

           });
        counter++;

    });
  }

  /////// function for view and like count///
  

</script>

  {

  	
    var s = $(".navbar-default");
    var pos = s.position();
    $(window).scroll(function() {
        var windowpos = $(window).scrollTop();
        if (windowpos >= 100) {
            s.addClass("topstick");
        } else {
            s.removeClass("topstick");
        }
    });

            a.removeClass("stick1");
        }
    });
